# Chamonix Valley Catalog Graph Remediation

Reconciles the exact prepared Chamonix Valley stay, access, terrain, pass, and trust graph. The completed schema-v5 discovery now records the full current Chamonix locality neighborhood, the prospective Argentière stay-destination boundary, the folded Le Savoy sector, and all permitted focus-to-regional context edges while leaving ordinary catalog remediation open.

| `stay_destination:argentiere` | `country` | `reviewed-no-change` | The official Argentière booking destination and official Chamonix Valley village page were checked and place the prospective market in the French Chamonix-Mont-Blanc destination context; France is the source-backed prospective country, while catalog materialization remains ordinary remediation. |
| `stay_destination:argentiere` | `latitude` | `reviewed-no-change` | OpenStreetMap node 285882627 was checked as the exact structured Argentière settlement object and supports prospective representative latitude 45.9838; catalog materialization remains ordinary remediation. |
| `stay_destination:argentiere` | `longitude` | `reviewed-no-change` | OpenStreetMap node 285882627 was checked as the exact structured Argentière settlement object and supports prospective representative longitude 6.9299; catalog materialization remains ordinary remediation. |
| `stay_destination:argentiere` | `name` | `reviewed-no-change` | The official booking destination page directly names the independent prospective stay market Argentière; catalog materialization remains ordinary remediation. |
| `stay_destination:argentiere` | `price_level` | `unresolved` | The official Argentière booking page was checked and exposes live per-stay examples, but it does not define a stable representative duration, season, or Snowcast price-level band. A bounded same-duration and same-season official accommodation sample mapped through the catalog price policy would resolve price_level. |
| `stay_destination:argentiere` | `region` | `reviewed-no-change` | The official Argentière and Chamonix Valley destination pages were checked together with the exact structured settlement object and place the prospective market in Haute-Savoie; catalog materialization remains ordinary remediation. |
